## 第 1 题（idx 0） · `medium` · CISA — ✓ exact 全对（jaccard 1.00）

**题干**

Given Androxgh0st's exploitation of CVE-2017-9841 via PHPUnit and targeting of .env files containing AWS credentials, which security control testing methodology would most effectively validate defenses against this threat while minimizing operational impact?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Penetration testing with a focus on Laravel application key exploitation 
- `B` Automated vulnerability scanning for CVE-2017-9841 with credential exposure checks 
- `C` Purple team exercise focusing on detection of Androxgh0st TTPs and .env file access （正确；★模型所选）
- `D` Security orchestration, automation, and response (SOAR) playbook testing for Androxgh0st IOCs 
- `E` Red team testing using PHPUnit exploit simulation with .env file credential harvesting 

**判定**：正确 `['C']` · 模型 `['C']` · jaccard 1.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
简要推理：题目核心是验证针对Androxgh0st利用PHPUnit（CVE-2017-9841）访问.env文件的防御能力，且要求“最小化运营影响”。紫队演练（C）专门聚焦于检测已知TTPs（如文件访问和凭据窃取），可针对现有检测规则进行验证，比红队或渗透测试更少干扰；而纯粹的漏洞扫描（B）可能无法全面验证检测和响应流程。A、D、E要么过度聚焦单一技术点，要么偏离“验证防御”的核心目标，或运营影响较大。

ANSWER: ["C"]
```

## 第 2 题（idx 1） · `medium` · CISA — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.00）

**题干**

Given the Androxgh0st threat actor's use of Active Scanning (T1595.002) to identify vulnerabilities, which vulnerability management strategy would most effectively prevent similar reconnaissance while minimizing operational disruption?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Implementing rate limiting on network scanning and vulnerability assessment tools 
- `B` Configuring firewalls to block suspicious scanning activity and known malicious IPs （正确）
- `C` Using a web application firewall (WAF) to detect and block scanning activity （正确）
- `D` Conducting regular vulnerability scans and penetration testing to identify weaknesses （★模型所选）
- `E` Implementing a bug bounty program to identify and remediate vulnerabilities 

**判定**：正确 `['B', 'C']` · 模型 `['D']` · jaccard 0.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
Androxgh0st uses active scanning to find vulnerabilities, so the most effective prevention is reducing the attack surface by identifying and fixing weaknesses before they are exploited. Regular vulnerability scans and penetration testing directly address this by finding and remediating the same vulnerabilities the actor seeks, without disrupting operations. Rate limiting or blocking scanning may slow reconnaissance but does not eliminate the underlying vulnerability risk.

ANSWER: ["D"]
```

## 第 3 题（idx 2） · `medium` · CISA — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.50）

**题干**

After detecting the ASPX Webshell (ConfigLogin.aspx) using YARA rule CISA_10430311_03 and identifying its obfuscation techniques to bypass security protocols, which combination of detection enhancement strategies would most effectively counter similar future threats that potentially utilize Meterpreter for post-exploitation activities?

- `A` Developing a comprehensive web application firewall (WAF) rule set to block suspicious ASPX file uploads 
- `B` Conducting regular web application vulnerability assessments to prevent initial compromise 
- `C` Integrating static and dynamic analysis for ASPX file inspection and monitoring for Meterpreter-related IOCs （正确；★模型所选）
- `D` Implementing machine learning-based anomaly detection for ASPX files and network traffic analysis for C2 communication 
- `E` Enhancing YARA rule CISA_10430311_03 with additional obfuscation detection patterns based on known Meterpreter samples （正确）

**判定**：正确 `['C', 'E']` · 模型 `['C']` · jaccard 0.50

**模型原始回答**

```text
题目核心是应对未来使用 Meterpreter 进行后渗透的威胁，且已知 ASPX webshell 通过混淆绕过检测。静态与动态结合分析 ASPX 文件，并监控 Meterpreter 相关 IOC，能直接针对 webshell 和后渗透阶段的行为链，比单纯扩展 YARA 规则或仅依赖 WAF/漏洞扫描更全面。知识条目中 T1027.010 支持混淆检测，T1056.003 支持对登录页文件改动的监控，均与题干场景相关。

ANSWER: ["C"]
```

## 第 4 题（idx 3） · `medium` · NSA — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.33）

**题干**

Given the SVR's use of password spraying and brute forcing (T1110) as initial infection vectors, which authentication security control combination would be most effective?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Implementing MFA with rate limiting on authentication attempts （正确；★模型所选）
- `B` Behavioral monitoring of authentication patterns （正确）
- `C` Account lockout policies without additional security controls 
- `D` Strong password policies without MFA 
- `E` Passwordless authentication with FIDO2 （正确）

**判定**：正确 `['A', 'B', 'E']` · 模型 `['A']` · jaccard 0.33

**模型原始回答**

```text
Password spraying specifically aims to avoid account lockouts by using one password across many accounts, so lockout alone (C) is insufficient, and strong passwords alone (D) do not stop valid-password spraying. MFA directly invalidates stolen or guessed credentials, while rate limiting slows brute force, making A the most directly effective. Behavioral monitoring (B) and FIDO2 passwordless (E) are strong but either secondary or not the primary mitigation for the threat described.

ANSWER: ["A"]
```

## 第 5 题（idx 4） · `medium` · NSA — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.33）

**题干**

Given APT29's adaptation of tactics for cloud access and their evolving TTPs, including the use of residential proxies (T1090.002), password spraying (T1110), and manipulating accounts (T1098.0), which comprehensive cloud threat detection strategies would be most effective against such sophisticated threat actor techniques?

- `A` Identity-centric monitoring with privileged access analysis and detection of unusual login locations （正确）
- `B` None of the above strategies would be effective against APT29's evolving TTPs 
- `C` Implementing cloud-native threat detection with behavioral analysis and monitoring for unusual authentication patterns （正确；★模型所选）
- `D` Cloud activity monitoring with anomaly detection and alerting on suspicious account activity （正确）
- `E` Traditional signature-based detection without cloud-specific controls or identity monitoring 

**判定**：正确 `['A', 'C', 'D']` · 模型 `['C']` · jaccard 0.33

**模型原始回答**

```text
APT29针对云环境的攻击强调身份与凭证滥用、隐蔽代理和账户操纵，因此检测需聚焦身份、登录行为、异常认证模式及账户权限变化。选项A、C、D均涉及身份或行为分析，但A仅强调登录位置，C更全面包含行为分析，D偏重通用异常告警；结合题目明确提到的代理、密码喷洒和账户操纵，C最直接覆盖所有技术，A和D虽合理但不如C全面，而B和E明显错误。知识条目中T1110、T1090.002、T1098.0均与题目直接相关，支持基于行为与身份的检测。

ANSWER: ["C"]
```

## 第 6 题（idx 5） · `medium` · NSA — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.50）

**题干**

Given the guidance from NCSC and CISA on protecting cloud assets from SVR and the importance of strong cyber security fundamentals, which identity and access management (IAM) control combination would be most effective against SVR's techniques?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Least privilege access with regular access reviews for cloud identities （正确）
- `B` Privileged access management (PAM) without behavioral monitoring 
- `C` Regular password rotation without MFA for cloud accounts 
- `D` Cloud identity governance without privileged account restrictions 
- `E` Implementing MFA with conditional access policies based on user behavior （正确；★模型所选）

**判定**：正确 `['A', 'E']` · 模型 `['E']` · jaccard 0.50

**模型原始回答**

```text
SVR 攻击常利用窃取的凭证结合 MFA 绕过或条件访问漏洞，因此需要 IAM 控制结合 MFA 与行为条件来应对。选项 E 直接匹配 SVR 对云身份滥用的手法，且 M1032（MFA）与 M1030（条件访问基于行为）在题干语境中相关。其他选项要么缺少 MFA（如 C），要么缺乏行为监控或特权限制（如 B、D），A 虽合理但不如 E 更直接针对 SVR 技术。

ANSWER: ["E"]
```

## 第 7 题（idx 6） · `medium` · CISA — ✓ exact 全对（jaccard 1.00）

**题干**

Given Volt Typhoon's observed use of SMBv1 for malicious activities, which SMB security measures would most effectively prevent or detect similar activity in the future?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Monitoring SMB traffic for suspicious activity （正确；★模型所选）
- `B` Implementing SMB signing to prevent tampering 
- `C` Disabling SMBv1 across the organization （正确；★模型所选）
- `D` Restricting SMB access to specific networks and systems 
- `E` Using SMB encryption to protect data in transit 

**判定**：正确 `['A', 'C']` · 模型 `['A', 'C']` · jaccard 1.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
Volt Typhoon’s use of SMBv1 indicates a legacy protocol weakness, so the most direct prevention is disabling SMBv1 entirely, while monitoring SMB traffic helps detect residual or future abuse. The other measures (signing, access restriction, encryption) are useful but less directly tied to eliminating the specific SMBv1 risk.

ANSWER: ["C","A"]
```

## 第 8 题（idx 7） · `medium` · CISA — ✓ exact 全对（jaccard 1.00）

**题干**

Given the PHP/Agent.NPM trojan's capabilities, including file upload, directory creation, and command execution via cmd.exe, and its obfuscation techniques using rot13, gzinflate, and base64_decode, which detection method would be most effective in identifying this malware while minimizing false positives?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` YARA rule-based detection with CISA_10443863_01 rule and additional context-aware rules （正确；★模型所选）
- `B` Network traffic monitoring for suspicious command and control communication 
- `C` Static analysis of PHP code for rot13, gzinflate, and base64_decode patterns 
- `D` Behavioral analysis focusing on cmd.exe execution patterns and file system modifications 
- `E` Signature-based detection using MD5/SHA256 hashes with frequent updates 

**判定**：正确 `['A']` · 模型 `['A']` · jaccard 1.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
该恶意软件使用PHP编写，主要功能包括文件上传、目录创建和通过cmd.exe执行命令，同时采用rot13、gzinflate和base64_decode进行混淆。静态分析检测这些混淆模式（C）虽有效，但易产生误报，因为合法PHP代码也可能使用这些函数；YARA规则检测（A）结合上下文规则可更精准识别已知样本变体并减少误报，且CISA规则提供了权威签名。因此，A是最直接匹配且能平衡有效性和误报率的方法。

ANSWER: ["A"]
```

## 第 9 题（idx 8） · `medium` · CISA — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.00）

**题干**

Following the analysis of sd.php and osker.aspx webshells, which hardening strategy would most effectively prevent their deployment and operation on Microsoft Exchange Servers and IIS services?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Implementing strict input validation and output encoding on all web applications （★模型所选）
- `B` Conducting regular security audits and vulnerability assessments 
- `C` Restricting users' ability to install and run unwanted software 
- `D` Using web application firewalls to detect and block suspicious traffic 
- `E` Regular patching and updating of Microsoft Exchange Servers and IIS services （正确）

**判定**：正确 `['E']` · 模型 `['A']` · jaccard 0.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
根据威胁背景，sd.php 和 osker.aspx 是 webshell，其部署和运行依赖于 Web 应用层的漏洞或文件上传缺陷，而非系统权限或网络流量过滤。IIS 组件的持久化技术（T1505.004）表明攻击者会利用 IIS 扩展或过滤器，但题目重点是“部署和操作”，最直接的阻止手段是加强对 Web 应用输入验证和输出编码，防止 webshell 上传或执行。其他选项（如补丁更新、WAF）有一定作用，但不如 A 针对 Web 应用本身的核心弱点。

ANSWER: ["A"]
```

## 第 10 题（idx 9） · `medium` · CISA — ✓ exact 全对（jaccard 1.00）

**题干**

Given the PHP/Agent.NPM trojan's reliance on the 'pass' parameter for authentication and its extensive capabilities including file management and command execution, which combination of mitigation strategies would most effectively prevent unauthorized access and limit potential damage?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Implementing strong password policies for all web applications （正确；★模型所选）
- `B` Using multi-factor authentication for all user accounts （正确；★模型所选）
- `C` None of the above 
- `D` Regularly updating and patching web application frameworks and related services 
- `E` Restricting access to sensitive areas of the web application using ACLs （正确；★模型所选）

**判定**：正确 `['A', 'B', 'E']` · 模型 `['A', 'B', 'E']` · jaccard 1.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
该木马依赖“pass”参数进行认证，且具备文件管理和命令执行能力，说明其核心风险在于绕过正常认证后直接控制Web服务器。加固认证（如强密码、MFA）和限制访问（ACL）能直接减少未授权访问面，而补丁更新主要预防已知漏洞，不能针对该木马的认证依赖。最有效的组合是强制认证加固（A、B）和访问控制（E），以阻断未经身份验证的利用及限制已授权账户的破坏范围。

ANSWER: ["A","B","E"]
```

**题干**

## 第 37 题（idx 36） · `medium` · CISA — ✓ exact 全对（jaccard 1.00）

**题干**

Given ALPHV Blackcat's use of Evilginx2 to obtain MFA credentials and session cookies, combined with their employment of Phishing for Information (T1598) via phone calls and SMS posing as IT staff, what defensive strategy would most effectively counter their initial access techniques while minimizing user disruption?

```text

FIDO/WebAuthn MFA resists credential theft and session replay, directly addressing Evilginx2's attack vector. Blocking unknown phone/SMS is disruptive and not fully effective, while training and verification steps help but are less direct than enforcing phishing-resistant MFA.

ANSWER: ["B"]
```

## 第 53 题（idx 52） · `medium` · CISA — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.50）

**题干**

If an organization had the described toolset (FRPC, ScanLine, and FRP) installed on their network, what correlation challenges might they face when attempting to link these events across their security infrastructure? Pick 3 that apply.

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Difficulty in correlating network logs due to the use of multiple protocols (TCP, UDP, HTTP, HTTPS) by the FRPC tool. （正确；★模型所选）
- `B` Challenges in identifying the source of the malicious activity due to the use of a reverse proxy by the FRPC tool. （正确；★模型所选）
- `C` Inability to detect the ScanLine tool's activity due to its use of a proprietary protocol. 
- `D` Complexity in analyzing the FRP tool's traffic due to its use of encryption. （★模型所选）
- `E` Issues in tracking the lateral movement of the threat actor due to the FRPC tool's ability to establish multiple proxy connections. （正确）
- `F` Problems in distinguishing between legitimate and malicious use of the tools due to their open-source nature. 

**判定**：正确 `['A', 'B', 'E']` · 模型 `['A', 'B', 'D']` · jaccard 0.50

**模型原始回答**

```text
FRPC 支持多协议（TCP/UDP/HTTP/HTTPS）和反向代理，这会导致网络日志关联困难，并增加识别恶意活动来源的难度；FRP 工具使用加密则增加了流量分析复杂性。ScanLine 的“proprietary protocol”说法不准确，且其通常使用标准协议；FRPC 的多代理连接与横向移动相关，但与题干强调的“关联挑战”直接匹配度不如 A、B、D；开源性质本身不构成关联挑战。

ANSWER: ["A","B","D"]
```

## 第 54 题（idx 53） · `medium` · CISA — ✓ exact 全对（jaccard 1.00）

**题干**

Volt Typhoon deployed several tools on the compromised system. Which of the following tools were used by Volt Typhoon for the specified purposes? Pick 3 that apply.

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` FRPC tool for port scanning 
- `B` ScanLine for opening a reverse proxy 
- `C` FRP for revealing servers behind a network firewall or obscured through NAT （正确；★模型所选）
- `D` FRPC tool for opening a reverse proxy between the compromised system and a Volt Typhoon C2 server （正确；★模型所选）
- `E` ScanLine for scanning open UDP and TCP ports （正确；★模型所选）

```text

ANSWER: ["C","D","E"]
```

## 第 112 题（idx 111） · `medium` · CISA — ✓ exact 全对（jaccard 1.00）

**题干**

Which of the following methods are used by the malware to execute commands received from the C2 server? Pick all that apply (3).

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` The malware decodes the hex-encoded commands and executes them directly. 
- `B` The malware uses cmd.exe to execute the decoded commands and stores the output in a text file. （正确；★模型所选）
- `C` The malware sends the output of the executed commands back to the C2 server. （正确；★模型所选）
- `D` The malware executes the commands using a custom-built interpreter. 
- `E` The malware uses PowerShell to execute the decoded commands. （正确；★模型所选）

**判定**：正确 `['B', 'C', 'E']` · 模型 `['B', 'C', 'E']` · jaccard 1.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
题目询问恶意软件如何执行从C2服务器接收的命令。结合常见恶意软件行为，解码命令后通常通过cmd.exe或PowerShell执行，并回传输出结果。检索到的知识条目均与本题无关，应忽略。

推理：A选项（直接解码执行）不典型，多数恶意软件通过系统shell执行；B（cmd.exe执行并保存输出）和E（PowerShell执行）是常见方式，但题目未明确说明输出存储；C（回传输出）是标准C2交互行为。根据题干“执行命令”的核心，最直接匹配的是B、C、E，但题目要求3个，且A、D明显不常见。综合常见威胁情报，恶意软件通常用cmd.exe或PowerShell执行解码命令，并将输出回传C2。因此选B、C、E。

但选项B提到“存储输出在文本文件”，可能并非标准回传方式。更稳妥的是C（回传输出）必然正确，A和D不典型，B和E中择一。鉴于题干未提PowerShell或cmd的偏好，且B明确描述了“存储输出”，而C是回传，二者可共存。但若只能选3个，则A、D排除，B、C、E均可能。考虑常见C2执行命令方式为cmd.exe或PowerShell，且回传输出，故选B、C、E。

然而，题目明确“执行命令”方法，回传输出（C）是后续操作，非执行方法。执行方法应为B和E（通过shell），但需3个，可能A也算（若解码后直接执行）。但A“直接执行”不常见。最合理为B、C、E。

最终选B、C、E。

ANSWER: ["B","C","E"]
```

**题干**

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` The malware's use of BIOS interrupt 13h in logical block addressing (LBA) mode allows it to access and overwrite data on the entire hard drive, leading to a complete denial of service. （正确；★模型所选）
- `B` The WhisperGate malware's destructive wiping operation is reversible, and the data can be recovered using specialized tools. 
- `C` The malware's decision to overwrite every 199th sector of the disk results in a relatively slow and targeted denial of service. （★模型所选）
- `D` The lack of a forced reboot by the bootloader installer suggests that the threat actor relied on other means to initiate the destructive wiping operation, potentially allowing other components to run before the denial of service occurs. （正确）
- `E` The WhisperGate malware's denial of service is limited to the master boot record (MBR) and does not affect the overall data stored on the disk. 
- `F` The malware's ransom note is an indication that the attackers intend to provide a decryption key or recovery method, mitigating the denial of service.

The lack of forced reboot means the payload may run later, aligning with D, but the question asks for likely true impact statements—A and C directly describe the wiping mechanism and its denial-of-service effect. B, E, F are false or unsupported.

```text
ANSWER: ["A","C"]
```

- `C` Utilizing a different credential harvesting tool instead of Mimikatz. （★模型所选）
- `D` Establishing a command and control channel using a different reverse proxy tool. 
- `E` Moving laterally using a different remote service, such as SSH instead of RDP. （正确）

```text
但题目说“可能考虑”的两个替代路径，且题干强调“维持持久化”和“收集凭据”两个目标，因此一个针对持久化（B），一个针对凭据（C）。A是初始访问，不符合已攻破前提；E是横向移动，不是持久化或凭据；D是C2，不直接相关。

```

- `B` They would probably use the same data exfiltration techniques, as the regulations do not directly impact their methods. （★模型所选）

- `E` They would likely switch to using different command line tools other than cURL and Rclone. 
- `F` They might increase the volume of data exfiltrated to overwhelm detection systems.

**模型原始回答**

```text
知识条目中T1567.001提到外渗到代码仓库，但题干明确提及cURL和Rclone，暗示原本使用这些工具，法规不会导致切换到完全不同的命令行工具。选项C（避免非GDPR合规的云存储）与法规约束下选择外渗目的地直接相关；选项B（使用相同技术，因法规不直接影响方法）符合攻击者务实视角，因为GDPR主要影响组织的数据保护实践，而非攻击者的技术选择。

因此，最直接匹配的是B和C。

ANSWER: ["B","C"]
```

## 第 216 题（idx 215） · `medium` · IC3 — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.33）

- `C` Use a different ransomware variant with similar TTPs. （★模型所选）

## 第 238 题（idx 237） · `medium` · IC3 — ✓ exact 全对（jaccard 1.00）

**题干**

Given Scattered Spider's use of social engineering techniques to bypass MFA and obtain credentials, which combination of authentication controls and security measures would most effectively counter their tactics while minimizing user disruption?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Implementing FIDO/WebAuthn with behavioral biometrics and conditional access policies （正确；★模型所选）
- `B` Enforcing PKI-based MFA with smart card authentication and restricted access controls 
- `C` Deploying passwordless authentication with device-bound passkeys and risk-based authentication （正确；★模型所选）
- `D` Combining SMS-based OTP with knowledge-based authentication and strict access controls 
- `E` Using time-based OTP with security awareness training and phishing-resistant MFA 

**判定**：正确 `['A', 'C']` · 模型 `['A', 'C']` · jaccard 1.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
Scattered Spider primarily uses social engineering to bypass MFA (e.g., MFA fatigue, SIM swapping, phishing) and to enroll attacker-controlled devices. The most effective countermeasures combine phishing-resistant, device-bound authentication (FIDO2/WebAuthn, passkeys) with risk-based/conditional access to detect anomalous behavior, while minimizing user disruption by avoiding legacy OTP or PKI complexity.  
Options A and C directly address these needs: A uses FIDO/WebAuthn plus behavioral biometrics and conditional access; C uses device-bound passkeys and risk-based authentication.  
B (PKI smart cards) is disruptive and less aligned with modern anti-phishing; D (SMS OTP) is vulnerable to SIM swapping and phishing; E (TOTP + training) still allows MFA fatigue and phishing attacks.

ANSWER: ["A","C"]
```

## 第 286 题（idx 285） · `medium` · CrowdStrike — ✓ exact 全对（jaccard 1.00）

**题干**

Given Rhadamanthys' use of WAV audio steganography and ECC encryption, what detection approach would most effectively identify related C2 communication while minimizing false positives?

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Memory analysis for Rhadamanthys loader artifacts and ECC encryption parameters 
- `B` Signature-based detection using YARA rules targeting Rhadamanthys steganography techniques 
- `C` Network traffic analysis focusing on HTTP header anomalies and ECC public key patterns （正确；★模型所选）
- `D` Behavioral analysis of processes communicating with C2 servers using ECC encryption 
- `E` File system monitoring for WAV files with unusual characteristics and ECC key material 

**判定**：正确 `['C']` · 模型 `['C']` · jaccard 1.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
Rhadamathys uses WAV steganography and ECC encryption specifically to hide C2 traffic in network communications. The most direct detection approach targets the network layer where the steganographic payload and ECC encryption are observable, and focuses on anomalies in HTTP headers (common C2 transport) and ECC public key patterns (which are distinctive). Memory, file system, or signature-based methods may detect artifacts but are less effective for ongoing C2 identification and risk false positives from benign media files or generic encryption.

ANSWER: ["C"]
```

- `B` Client verification is based solely on process name matching

These two mechanisms work together to ensure the client is the genuine ProcessHacker process, not an impostor.

```text
ANSWER: ["A","D"]
```

## 第 330 题（idx 329） · `medium` · CrowdStrike — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.33）

**题干**

How might adversaries evolve their techniques to bypass the defenses against Kerberoasting mentioned in the report? Pick 2 that apply.

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` By using alternative authentication protocols that are not monitored as closely as Kerberos. （正确；★模型所选）
- `B` By exploiting vulnerabilities in the Kerberos protocol that are not yet patched. （正确）
- `C` By using encryption types that are not vulnerable to cracking, such as AES. （★模型所选）
- `D` By reducing the frequency of Kerberoasting attacks to avoid detection. 
- `E` By targeting non-Windows environments where Kerberoasting is not applicable. 

**判定**：正确 `['A', 'B']` · 模型 `['A', 'C']` · jaccard 0.33

**模型原始回答**

```text
推理：Kerberoasting 依赖 Kerberos TGS 票据的离线破解，防御通常通过监控 RC4 加密的 TGS 请求（Event 4769）和异常票数来检测。攻击者可通过使用监控较少的替代认证协议（A）绕开 Kerberos 专属检测，或改用不易破解的 AES 加密（C）使离线破解失效（但实际 AES 通常不可破解，因此攻击者可能转向 A 而非 C——不过题目问“绕过防御”，C 是直接降低破解成功率，但并非绕过检测）。在选项中，A 明确绕过基于 Kerberos 的监控，C 虽然降低破解成功率，但更属于防御已升级导致攻击者转向其他协议或加密方式，故 A 最直接，C 也作为技术演进可能（但实际 AES 难破解，攻击者不会主动选 C，除非混淆）。综合题目“bypass the defenses”，A 和 C 都是可行的技术演进，但 C 更偏向“避免破解”而非“绕过检测”。严格按题干“bypass defenses against Kerberoasting”，A 和 C 都算绕过（C 使破解无效，从而绕过防御）。故选 A、C。

ANSWER: ["A","C"]
```

## 第 333 题（idx 332） · `medium` · CrowdStrike — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.33）

**题干**

How might the adversary evolve their techniques to bypass the specific defenses that could be implemented to prevent the KProcessHacker driver from being exploited? Pick 2 that apply.

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` By using a different legitimate tool instead of ProcessHacker （★模型所选）
- `B` By implementing a kernel-mode driver to monitor and block suspicious IOCTL requests to the KProcessHacker service 
- `C` By signing their own version of ProcessHacker with a valid digital certificate to pass the verification checks （正确；★模型所选）
- `D` By using user-mode API hooking to intercept and manipulate the calls to KProcessHacker （正确）
- `E` By exploiting a vulnerability in the Windows kernel to gain elevated privileges 

**判定**：正确 `['C', 'D']` · 模型 `['A', 'C']` · jaccard 0.33

**模型原始回答**

```text
KProcessHacker is a legitimate kernel driver that adversaries often abuse. If defenses block its exploitation (e.g., by monitoring IOCTLs or verifying signatures), the adversary would likely switch to another legitimate tool (A) or sign their own malicious version to pass signature checks (C). Implementing a kernel driver to block suspicious IOCTLs is a defensive action, not an evasion technique. User-mode hooking would not help bypass kernel-level driver checks. Exploiting a Windows kernel vulnerability is a broader escalation, not directly related to bypassing specific defenses for KProcessHacker.

ANSWER: ["A","C"]
```

## 第 334 题（idx 333） · `medium` · CrowdStrike — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.60）

**题干**

Which of the following statements regarding the Rhadamanthys payload decryption process are accurate? Pick all that apply (4).

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` The RC4 key is derived solely from the client private key. 
- `B` The shared secret is computed using the client private key and the server public key. （正确；★模型所选）
- `C` The payload is decrypted using AES encryption. （★模型所选）
- `D` The RC4 key is obtained by hashing the shared secret with a key salt. （正确；★模型所选）
- `E` The decryption process involves verifying the SHA256 hash of the decrypted payload for WAV files. （正确；★模型所选）
- `F` The decryption process involves verifying the SHA1 hash of the decrypted payload for JPEG files. （正确）

**判定**：正确 `['B', 'D', 'E', 'F']` · 模型 `['B', 'C', 'D', 'E']` · jaccard 0.60

**模型原始回答**

```text
Rhadamanthys 是已知的窃密恶意软件，其载荷解密过程通常涉及基于 ECDH 的共享密钥计算（客户端私钥与服务器公钥），然后通过哈希共享密钥加盐派生 RC4 密钥，最后用 RC4 解密载荷，并验证 SHA256 哈希以确认完整性（针对 WAV 文件）。A 选项错误，因为 RC4 密钥并非仅由客户端私钥推导；E 选项正确，F 选项错误（应为 SHA256 而非 SHA1）。B、C、D 均符合已知解密流程。

ANSWER: ["B","C","D","E"]
```

## 第 351 题（idx 350） · `medium` · IC3 — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.00）

**题干**

Which of the following measures could potentially hinder the threat actor's ability to maintain persistence in the compromised network? Select the best option

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` Implementing a web application firewall (WAF) to block traffic to known Ngrok domains （正确）
- `B` Disabling RDP access for all user accounts 
- `C` Enabling Credential Guard for Windows 10 and Server 2016 （★模型所选）
- `D` Regularly updating and patching VMware Horizon servers 
- `E` Conducting regular audits of administrator accounts and permissions 

**判定**：正确 `['A']` · 模型 `['C']` · jaccard 0.00

**模型原始回答**

```text
题目问的是“阻碍威胁行为者在被入侵网络中维持持久性”的措施。持久性通常涉及账户权限、后门、脚本或系统配置的滥用。选项A（WAF阻止Ngrok域名）主要影响C2通信，而非持久性本身；选项B（禁用RDP）可能影响远程访问，但攻击者可用其他方式持久；选项C（Credential Guard）保护凭据，间接阻止横向移动和持久性（如利用凭据创建计划任务）；选项D（修补VMware）针对初始漏洞利用，与持久性无直接关系；选项E（审计管理员账户和权限）是检测和移除持久性的有效措施，但更偏向事后发现。最直接阻断持久性机制的是C（防止凭据盗窃用于持久化）和E（发现并清除隐藏账户/权限提升）。然而题目强调“维持持久性”，审计能发现异常持久性，但不如禁用凭据滥用直接。根据ATT&CK知识中持久性常依赖登录脚本（如T1037.003）或凭据重用，Credential Guard更对症。但E更能覆盖多种持久性（如隐藏管理员）。综合考虑，C和E均合理，但C是主动技术缓解，E是被动审计。题目要求“最佳”，单选中C最直接阻断凭据相关持久性。但多选可能同时包含C和E。基于常见考试逻辑，优先选择C作为最直接措施。

ANSWER: ["C"]
```

## 第 430 题（idx 430） · `medium` · IC3 — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.20）

**题干**

Which of the following technical details provide the strongest evidence for attributing the exploitation of CVE-2023-42793 to the Russian Foreign Intelligence Service (SVR)? Pick 3 that apply.

**选项**（正确 / ★=模型所选）：
- `A` The use of GraphicalProton HTTPs variant, which is a custom tool used by the SVR. （正确；★模型所选）
- `B` The exploitation of CVE-2023-42793 to gain initial access to JetBrains TeamCity servers. （正确）
- `C` The use of EDRSandBlast to disable endpoint detection and response (EDR) and antivirus (AV) software. （★模型所选）
- `D` The modification of the NoLMHash registry key to facilitate privilege escalation. 
- `E` The use of Microsoft OneDrive and Dropbox cloud services as a covert C2 channel. （正确）
- `F` The use of Rsockstun, a modified open-source reverse socks tunneler, to establish a tunnel to the C2 infrastructure. （★模型所选）

**判定**：正确 `['A', 'B', 'E']` · 模型 `['A', 'C', 'F']` · jaccard 0.20

**模型原始回答**


## 第 460 题（idx 460） · `medium` · CrowdStrike — ✗ 未全对（jaccard 0.00）

- `C` Deployment of identical JavaScript versions across all domains for consistency （★模型所选）

**模型原始回答**

```text
题干询问的是b1ack在分析assets.sagepay[.]com相关域名基础设施时暴露的操作安全（OPSEC）模式。该背景涉及域名基础设施的横向对比，而非技术战术或地理位置。  
根据威胁情报常识，使用主域名assets.sagepay[.]com作为主要域名，其他域名作为备份，是一种常见的基础设施冗余模式，但“暴露OPSEC”通常指攻击者错误地复用相同资源或模式，导致关联性被揭示。

然而，题目强调“technical pattern”，且结合相似域名，最明显的OPSEC失误是不同域名使用相同JavaScript版本（C），因为攻击者可能忽略版本差异化，从而被检测人员通过哈希或内容关联识别。B选项“不同版本用于弹性”是合理防御行为，不符合OPSEC失误。  
因此，最直接匹配OPSEC暴露的是C。

ANSWER: ["C"]
```
